{"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"05c76c4819ea5143af8807eb95f25282","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"call_function_67t23wrromlf_2","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/phase_picker.py","content":"import numpy as np\nfrom scipy import signal\nfrom scipy.stats import kurtosis\nimport os\n\ndef preprocess_data(data):\n    \"\"\"Remove mean and apply bandpass filter.\"\"\"\n    # Remove mean\n    data_demean = data - np.mean(data, axis=0, keepdims=True)\n\n    # Apply bandpass filter (1-20 Hz typical for earthquake detection)\n    # Calculate sampling rate from dt (will be passed as parameter)\n    return data_demean\n\ndef sta_lta_picker(trace, sta_len, lta_len, threshold):\n    \"\"\"\n    STA/LTA picker for phase detection.\n    trace: input signal\n    sta_len: short-term average window length (samples)\n    lta_len: long-term average window length (samples)\n    threshold: trigger threshold\n    \"\"\"\n    # Calculate STA and LTA\n    sta = np.zeros(len(trace))\n    lta = np.ones(len(trace))  # Initialize LTA with 1s\n\n    # Compute STA\n    for i in range(sta_len, len(trace)):\n        sta[i] = np.sum(trace[i-sta_len:i]**2) / sta_len\n\n    # Compute LTA\n    for i in range(lta_len, len(trace)):\n        lta[i] = np.sum(trace[i-lta_len:i]**2) / lta_len\n\n    # Avoid division by zero\n    lta[lta == 0] = 1e-10\n\n    # Calculate characteristic function\n    char_func = sta / (lta + 1e-10)\n\n    # Find triggers\n    triggers = []\n    for i in range(1, len(char_func)):\n        if char_func[i] > threshold and char_func[i-1] <= threshold:\n            triggers.append(i)\n\n    return triggers\n\ndef kurtosis_picker(trace, window, threshold):\n    \"\"\"\n    Kurtosis-based picker.\n    Detects changes in signal statistics (Gaussianity).\n    \"\"\"\n    kurt = np.zeros(len(trace))\n    half_window = window // 2\n\n    for i in range(half_window, len(trace) - half_window):\n        window_data = trace[i-half_window:i+half_window]\n        kurt[i] = kurtosis(window_data)\n\n    # Find peaks in kurtosis\n    peaks, _ = signal.find_peaks(kurt, height=threshold)\n\n    return peaks\n\ndef energy_picker(trace, window, threshold_factor=2.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Energy-based picker.\n    Detects sudden increases in signal energy.\n    \"\"\"\n    energy = np.zeros(len(trace))\n    half_window = window // 2\n\n    for i in range(half_window, len(trace) - half_window):\n        energy[i] = np.sum(trace[i-half_window:i+half_window]**2)\n\n    # Smooth energy\n    energy_smooth = np.convolve(energy, np.ones(50)/50, mode='same')\n\n    # Find significant increases\n    mean_energy = np.mean(energy_smooth[:100])  # Initial noise level\n    threshold = mean_energy * threshold_factor\n\n    triggers = []\n    for i in range(10, len(energy_smooth)):\n        if energy_smooth[i] > threshold and energy_smooth[i-5:i].max() < threshold:\n            triggers.append(i)\n\n    return triggers\n\ndef pick_p_wave(vertical_trace, dt, data_shape):\n    \"\"\"\n    Pick P-wave using multiple methods and combine results.\n    vertical_trace: Z channel data\n    dt: sampling interval\n    \"\"\"\n    # Preprocess\n    vertical_trace = vertical_trace - np.mean(vertical_trace)\n    vertical_trace = vertical_trace / (np.std(vertical_trace) + 1e-10)\n\n    # Method 1: STA/LTA on vertical channel\n    # Use shorter window for P-waves (they are typically clearer)\n    sta_len = max(10, int(0.5 / dt))  # 0.5 seconds\n    lta_len = max(50, int(5.0 / dt))  # 5 seconds\n    sta_lta_picks = sta_lta_picker(vertical_trace, sta_len, lta_len, threshold=3.0)\n\n    # Method 2: Kurtosis on vertical channel\n    kurt_picks = kurtosis_picker(vertical_trace, window=50, threshold=0.5)\n\n    # Method 3: Energy-based on vertical channel\n    energy_picks = energy_picker(vertical_trace, window=20, threshold_factor=3.0)\n\n    # Combine and filter picks\n    all_picks = sta_lta_picks + kurt_picks + energy_picks\n\n    if len(all_picks) == 0:\n        return []\n\n    # Remove duplicates and sort\n    all_picks = sorted(set(all_picks))\n\n    # Filter picks: should be in first 40% of trace (P-waves arrive early)\n    max_pick_idx = int(0.4 * len(vertical_trace))\n    filtered_picks = [p for p in all_picks if p < max_pick_idx and p > 100]\n\n    # If no picks, try with lower threshold\n    if len(filtered_picks) == 0:\n        sta_lta_picks = sta_lta_picker(vertical_trace, sta_len, lta_len, threshold=2.0)\n        energy_picks = energy_picker(vertical_trace, window=20, threshold_factor=2.0)\n        all_picks = sta_lta_picks + energy_picks\n        if len(all_picks) > 0:\n            filtered_picks = [max(100, min(all_picks))]\n\n    return filtered_picks\n\ndef pick_s_wave(horizontal_traces, vertical_trace, dt):\n    \"\"\"\n    Pick S-wave using horizontal channels.\n    horizontal_traces: E and N channel data\n    vertical_trace: Z channel data (for reference)\n    dt: sampling interval\n    \"\"\"\n    # Combine horizontal traces\n    horizontal_combined = np.sqrt(horizontal_traces[:, 0]**2 + horizontal_traces[:, 1]**2)\n\n    # Preprocess\n    horizontal_combined = horizontal_combined - np.mean(horizontal_combined)\n    horizontal_combined = horizontal_combined / (np.std(horizontal_combined) + 1e-10)\n\n    # Method 1: STA/LTA on horizontal channels\n    sta_len = max(10, int(1.0 / dt))  # 1 second\n    lta_len = max(50, int(10.0 / dt))  # 10 seconds\n    sta_lta_picks = sta_lta_picker(horizontal_combined, sta_len, lta_len, threshold=2.5)\n\n    # Method 2: Kurtosis on horizontal channels\n    kurt_picks = kurtosis_picker(horizontal_combined, window=80, threshold=0.3)\n\n    # Method 3: Energy-based on horizontal channels\n    energy_picks = energy_picker(horizontal_combined, window=30, threshold_factor=2.5)\n\n    # Combine picks\n    all_picks = sta_lta_picks + kurt_picks + energy_picks\n\n    if len(all_picks) == 0:\n        return []\n\n    # Remove duplicates and sort\n    all_picks = sorted(set(all_picks))\n\n    # Filter picks: S-waves typically arrive after P-waves\n    # Look in the middle to later part of the trace\n    min_pick_idx = int(0.15 * len(horizontal_combined))  # At least some time after P-wave\n    max_pick_idx = int(0.85 * len(horizontal_combined))\n    filtered_picks = [p for p in all_picks if min_pick_idx <= p < max_pick_idx]\n\n    # Remove mean\n    data_demean = data - np.mean(data, axis=0, keepdims=True)\n\n    # Apply bandpass filter (1-20 Hz typical for earthquake detection)\n    # Calculate sampling rate from dt (will be passed as parameter)\n    return data_demean\n\ndef sta_lta_picker(trace, sta_len, lta_len, threshold):\n    \"\"\"\n    STA/LTA picker for phase detection.\n    trace: input signal\n    sta_len: short-term average window length (samples)\n    lta_len: long-term average window length (samples)\n    threshold: trigger threshold\n    \"\"\"\n    # Calculate STA and LTA\n    sta = np.zeros(len(trace))\n    lta = np.ones(len(trace))  # Initialize LTA with 1s\n\n    # Compute STA\n    for i in range(sta_len, len(trace)):\n        sta[i] = np.sum(trace[i-sta_len:i]**2) / sta_len\n\n    # Compute LTA\n    for i in range(lta_len, len(trace)):\n        lta[i] = np.sum(trace[i-lta_len:i]**2) / lta_len\n\n    # Avoid division by zero\n    lta[lta == 0] = 1e-10\n\n    # Calculate characteristic function\n    char_func = sta / (lta + 1e-10)\n\n    # Find triggers\n    triggers = []\n    for i in range(1, len(char_func)):\n        if char_func[i] > threshold and char_func[i-1] <= threshold:\n            triggers.append(i)\n\n    return triggers\n\ndef kurtosis_picker(trace, window, threshold):\n    \"\"\"\n    Kurtosis-based picker.\n    Detects changes in signal statistics (Gaussianity).\n    \"\"\"\n    kurt = np.zeros(len(trace))\n    half_window = window // 2\n\n    for i in range(half_window, len(trace) - half_window):\n        window_data = trace[i-half_window:i+half_window]\n        kurt[i] = kurtosis(window_data)\n\n    # Find peaks in kurtosis\n    peaks, _ = signal.find_peaks(kurt, height=threshold)\n\n    return peaks\n\ndef energy_picker(trace, window, threshold_factor=2.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Energy-based picker.\n    Detects sudden increases in signal energy.\n    \"\"\"\n    energy = np.zeros(len(trace))\n    half_window = window // 2\n\n    for i in range(half_window, len(trace) - half_window):\n        energy[i] = np.sum(trace[i-half_window:i+half_window]**2)\n\n    # Smooth energy\n    energy_smooth = np.convolve(energy, np.ones(50)/50, mode='same')\n\n    # Find significant increases\n    mean_energy = np.mean(energy_smooth[:100])  # Initial noise level\n    threshold = mean_energy * threshold_factor\n\n    triggers = []\n    for i in range(10, len(energy_smooth)):\n        if energy_smooth[i] > threshold and energy_smooth[i-5:i].max() < threshold:\n            triggers.append(i)\n\n    return triggers\n\ndef pick_p_wave(vertical_trace, dt, data_shape):\n    \"\"\"\n    Pick P-wave using multiple methods and combine results.\n    vertical_trace: Z channel data\n    dt: sampling interval\n    \"\"\"\n    # Preprocess\n    vertical_trace = vertical_trace - np.mean(vertical_trace)\n    vertical_trace = vertical_trace / (np.std(vertical_trace) + 1e-10)\n\n    # Method 1: STA/LTA on vertical channel\n    # Use shorter window for P-waves (they are typically clearer)\n    sta_len = max(10, int(0.5 / dt))  # 0.5 seconds\n    lta_len = max(50, int(5.0 / dt))  # 5 seconds\n    sta_lta_picks = sta_lta_picker(vertical_trace, sta_len, lta_len, threshold=3.0)\n\n    # Method 2: Kurtosis on vertical channel\n    kurt_picks = kurtosis_picker(vertical_trace, window=50, threshold=0.5)\n\n    # Method 3: Energy-based on vertical channel\n    energy_picks = energy_picker(vertical_trace, window=20, threshold_factor=3.0)\n\n    # Combine and filter picks\n    all_picks = sta_lta_picks + kurt_picks + energy_picks\n\n    if len(all_picks) == 0:\n        return []\n\n    # Remove duplicates and sort\n    all_picks = sorted(set(all_picks))\n\n    # Filter picks: should be in first 40% of trace (P-waves arrive early)\n    max_pick_idx = int(0.4 * len(vertical_trace))\n    filtered_picks = [p for p in all_picks if p < max_pick_idx and p > 100]\n\n    # If no picks, try with lower threshold\n    if len(filtered_picks) == 0:\n        sta_lta_picks = sta_lta_picker(vertical_trace, sta_len, lta_len, threshold=2.0)\n        energy_picks = energy_picker(vertical_trace, window=20, threshold_factor=2.0)\n        all_picks = sta_lta_picks + energy_picks\n        if len(all_picks) > 0:\n            filtered_picks = [max(100, min(all_picks))]\n\n    return filtered_picks\n\ndef pick_s_wave(horizontal_traces, vertical_trace, dt):\n    \"\"\"\n    Pick S-wave using horizontal channels.\n    horizontal_traces: E and N channel data\n    vertical_trace: Z channel data (for reference)\n    dt: sampling interval\n    \"\"\"\n    # Combine horizontal traces\n    horizontal_combined = np.sqrt(horizontal_traces[:, 0]**2 + horizontal_traces[:, 1]**2)\n\n    # Preprocess\n    horizontal_combined = horizontal_combined - np.mean(horizontal_combined)\n    horizontal_combined = horizontal_combined / (np.std(horizontal_combined) + 1e-10)\n\n    # Method 1: STA/LTA on horizontal channels\n    sta_len = max(10, int(1.0 / dt))  # 1 second\n    lta_len = max(50, int(10.0 / dt))  # 10 seconds\n    sta_lta_picks = sta_lta_picker(horizontal_combined, sta_len, lta_len, threshold=2.5)\n\n    # Method 2: Kurtosis on horizontal channels\n    kurt_picks = kurtosis_picker(horizontal_combined, window=80, threshold=0.3)\n\n    # Method 3: Energy-based on horizontal channels\n    energy_picks = energy_picker(horizontal_combined, window=30, threshold_factor=2.5)\n\n    # Combine picks\n    all_picks = sta_lta_picks + kurt_picks + energy_picks\n\n    if len(all_picks) == 0:\n        return []\n\n    # Remove duplicates and sort\n    all_picks = sorted(set(all_picks))\n\n    # Filter picks: S-waves typically arrive after P-waves\n    # Look in the middle to later part of the trace\n    min_pick_idx = int(0.15 * len(horizontal_combined))  # At least some time after P-wave\n    max_pick_idx = int(0.85 * len(horizontal_combined))\n    filtered_picks = [p for p in all_picks if min_pick_idx <= p < max_pick_idx]\n\n    # Smooth energy\n    energy_smooth = np.convolve(energy, np.ones(50)/50, mode='same')\n\n    # Find significant increases\n    mean_energy = np.mean(energy_smooth[:100])  # Initial noise level\n    threshold = mean_energy * threshold_factor\n\n    triggers = []\n    for i in range(10, len(energy_smooth)):\n        if energy_smooth[i] > threshold and energy_smooth[i-5:i].max() < threshold:\n            triggers.append(i)\n\n    return triggers\n\ndef pick_p_wave(vertical_trace, dt, data_shape):\n    \"\"\"\n    Pick P-wave using multiple methods and combine results.\n    vertical_trace: Z channel data\n    dt: sampling interval\n    \"\"\"\n    # Preprocess\n    vertical_trace = vertical_trace - np.mean(vertical_trace)\n    vertical_trace = vertical_trace / (np.std(vertical_trace) + 1e-10)\n\n    # Method 1: STA/LTA on vertical channel\n    # Use shorter window for P-waves (they are typically clearer)\n    sta_len = max(10, int(0.5 / dt))  # 0.5 seconds\n    lta_len = max(50, int(5.0 / dt))  # 5 seconds\n    sta_lta_picks = sta_lta_picker(vertical_trace, sta_len, lta_len, threshold=3.0)\n\n    # Method 2: Kurtosis on vertical channel\n    kurt_picks = kurtosis_picker(vertical_trace, window=50, threshold=0.5)\n\n    # Method 3: Energy-based on vertical channel\n    energy_picks = energy_picker(vertical_trace, window=20, threshold_factor=3.0)\n\n    # Combine and filter picks\n    all_picks = sta_lta_picks + kurt_picks + energy_picks\n\n    if len(all_picks) == 0:\n        return []\n\n    # Remove duplicates and sort\n    all_picks = sorted(set(all_picks))\n\n    # Filter picks: should be in first 40% of trace (P-waves arrive early)\n    max_pick_idx = int(0.4 * len(vertical_trace))\n    filtered_picks = [p for p in all_picks if p < max_pick_idx and p > 100]\n\n    # If no picks, try with lower threshold\n    if len(filtered_picks) == 0:\n        sta_lta_picks = sta_lta_picker(vertical_trace, sta_len, lta_len, threshold=2.0)\n        energy_picks = energy_picker(vertical_trace, window=20, threshold_factor=2.0)\n        all_picks = sta_lta_picks + energy_picks\n        if len(all_picks) > 0:\n            filtered_picks = [max(100, min(all_picks))]\n\n    return filtered_picks\n\ndef pick_s_wave(horizontal_traces, vertical_trace, dt):\n    \"\"\"\n    Pick S-wave using horizontal channels.\n    horizontal_traces: E and N channel data\n    vertical_trace: Z channel data (for reference)\n    dt: sampling interval\n    \"\"\"\n    # Combine horizontal traces\n    horizontal_combined = np.sqrt(horizontal_traces[:, 0]**2 + horizontal_traces[:, 1]**2)\n\n    # Preprocess\n    horizontal_combined = horizontal_combined - np.mean(horizontal_combined)\n    horizontal_combined = horizontal_combined / (np.std(horizontal_combined) + 1e-10)\n\n    # Method 1: STA/LTA on horizontal channels\n    sta_len = max(10, int(1.0 / dt))  # 1 second\n    lta_len = max(50, int(10.0 / dt))  # 10 seconds\n    sta_lta_picks = sta_lta_picker(horizontal_combined, sta_len, lta_len, threshold=2.5)\n\n    # Method 2: Kurtosis on horizontal channels\n    kurt_picks = kurtosis_picker(horizontal_combined, window=80, threshold=0.3)\n\n    # Method 3: Energy-based on horizontal channels\n    energy_picks = energy_picker(horizontal_combined, window=30, threshold_factor=2.5)\n\n    # Combine picks\n    all_picks = sta_lta_picks + kurt_picks + energy_picks\n\n    if len(all_picks) == 0:\n        return []\n\n    # Remove duplicates and sort\n    all_picks = sorted(set(all_picks))\n\n    # Filter picks: S-waves typically arrive after P-waves\n    # Look in the middle to later part of the trace\n    min_pick_idx = int(0.15 * len(horizontal_combined))  # At least some time after P-wave\n    max_pick_idx = int(0.85 * len(horizontal_combined))\n    filtered_picks = [p for p in all_picks if min_pick_idx <= p < max_pick_idx]\n\n    # If no picks found in expected range, use earliest pick\n    if len(filtered_picks) == 0 and len(all_picks) > 0:\n        filtered_picks = [p for p in all_picks if p > 100]\n\n    return filtered_picks\n\ndef process_trace(file_path):\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a single trace file and return picks.\n    Returns: list of tuples (phase_type, pick_index)\n    \"\"\"\n    data = np.load(file_path)\n    trace_data = data['data']\n    dt = data['dt']\n\n    picks = []\n\n    # Extract channels (DPE, DPN, DPZ)\n    channels = data['channels'].split(',')\n\n    # Find channel indices\n    e_idx = channels.index('DPE') if 'DPE' in channels else 0\n    n_idx = channels.index('DPN') if 'DPN' in channels else 1\n    z_idx = channels.index('DPZ') if 'DPZ' in channels else 2\n\n    # Extract traces\n    vertical_trace = trace_data[:, z_idx]\n    horizontal_traces = trace_data[:, [e_idx, n_idx]]\n\n    # Pick P-wave (use vertical channel)\n    p_picks = pick_p_wave(vertical_trace, dt, trace_data.shape)\n\n    # Pick S-wave (use horizontal channels)\n    s_picks = pick_s_wave(horizontal_traces, vertical_trace, dt)\n\n    # Store picks\n    for pick_idx in p_picks:\n        picks.append(('P', pick_idx))\n\n    for pick_idx in s_picks:\n        picks.append(('S', pick_idx))\n\n    return picks\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main function to process all traces.\"\"\"\n    data_dir = '/root/data'\n    results = []\n\n    # Get all npz files\n    npz_files = sorted([f for f in os.listdir(data_dir) if f.endswith('.npz')])\n\n    print(f\"Processing {len(npz_files)} files...\")\n\n    for i, filename in enumerate(npz_files):\n        if (i + 1) % 10 == 0:\n            print(f\"  Processed {i + 1}/{len(npz_files)} files...\")\n\n        file_path = os.path.join(data_dir, filename)\n        picks = process_trace(file_path)\n\n        # Add to results\n        for phase, pick_idx in picks:\n            results.append({\n                'file_name': filename,\n                'phase': phase,\n                'pick_idx': pick_idx\n            })\n\n    # Write results to CSV\n    output_file = '/root/results.csv'\n    import csv\n\n    with open(output_file, 'w', newline='') as f:\n        writer = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=['file_name', 'phase', 'pick_idx'])\n        writer.writeheader()\n        writer.writerows(results)\n\n    print(f\"\\nResults written to {output_file}\")\n    print(f\"Total picks: {len(results)}\")\n\n    # Extract traces\n    vertical_trace = trace_data[:, z_idx]\n    horizontal_traces = trace_data[:, [e_idx, n_idx]]\n\n    # Pick P-wave (use vertical channel)\n    p_picks = pick_p_wave(vertical_trace, dt, trace_data.shape)\n\n    # Extract traces\n    vertical_trace = trace_data[:, z_idx]\n    horizontal_traces = trace_data[:, [e_idx, n_idx]]\n\n    # Pick P-wave (use vertical channel)\n    p_picks = pick_p_wave(vertical_trace, dt, trace_data.shape)\n\n    # {"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"05c76da044eba304720c4acfe29c19ad","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"call_function_76c85e9syqyc_1","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/phase_picker_v2.py","content":"import numpy as np\nfrom scipy import signal\nimport os\nimport csv\n\ndef enhanced_sta_lta(trace, sta_samples=50, lta_samples=500, threshold_p=3.0, threshold_s=2.5):\n    \"\"\"\n    Enhanced STA/LTA picker for both P and S waves.\n    Optimized for speed and accuracy.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Compute squared trace to emphasize arrivals\n    trace_sq = trace ** 2\n\n    # Compute STA and LTA using convolution for speed\n    sta_kernel = np.ones(sta_samples) / sta_samples\n    lta_kernel = np.ones(lta_samples) / lta_samples\n\n    sta = np.convolve(trace_sq, sta_kernel, mode='same')\n    lta = np.convolve(trace_sq, lta_kernel, mode='same')\n\n    # Avoid division by zero\n    lta[lta < 1e-8] = 1e-8\n\n    # Characteristic function\n    char_func = sta / lta\n\n    # Find peaks with minimum distance to avoid multiple picks of same phase\n    min_dist = max(sta_samples, lta_samples) // 4\n\n    # Use different thresholds for P and S waves\n    threshold = threshold_p if sta_samples < 100 else threshold_s\n\n    peaks, _ = signal.find_peaks(char_func, height=threshold, distance=min_dist)\n\n    return peaks\n\ndef adaptive_picker(trace, dt, phase_type='P'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Adaptive picker that adjusts thresholds based on signal characteristics.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Normalize trace\n    trace = trace - np.mean(trace)\n    std_val = np.std(trace)\n    if std_val < 1e-10:\n        return []\n    trace = trace / std_val\n\n    # For P-waves, use vertical channel characteristics\n    if phase_type == 'P':\n        # Shorter windows for P-waves (they have higher frequencies)\n        sta = max(5, int(0.3 / dt))\n        lta = max(50, int(3.0 / dt))\n        threshold = 2.5\n        max_idx = int(0.5 * len(trace))\n    else:  # S-waves\n        # Longer windows for S-waves (lower frequencies)\n        sta = max(10, int(0.8 / dt))\n        lta = max(100, int(8.0 / dt))\n        threshold = 2.0\n        min_idx = int(0.2 * len(trace))\n        max_idx = int(0.9 * len(trace))\n\n    # Get picks using STA/LTA\n    picks = enhanced_sta_lta(trace, sta_samples=sta, lta_samples=lta,\n                            threshold_p=threshold, threshold_s=threshold)\n\n    # Filter picks based on phase type\n    if phase_type == 'P':\n        # P-waves arrive early\n        picks = [p for p in picks if 100 < p < max_idx]\n    else:\n        # S-waves arrive after P-waves\n        picks = [p for p in picks if min_idx < p < max_idx]\n\n    return picks\n\ndef process_trace(file_path):\n    \"\"\"Process a single trace file and return picks.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        data = np.load(file_path)\n        trace_data = data['data']\n        dt = float(data['dt'])\n        channels = str(data['channels']).split(',')\n\n        # Find channel indices\n        try:\n            z_idx = channels.index('DPZ') if 'DPZ' in channels else 2\n        except ValueError:\n            z_idx = 2\n\n        # Try to find horizontal channels\n        try:\n            e_idx = channels.index('DPE') if 'DPE' in channels else 0\n            n_idx = channels.index('DPN') if 'DPN' in channels else 1\n        except ValueError:\n            e_idx = 0\n            n_idx = 1\n\n        picks = []\n\n        # Pick P-wave from vertical channel\n        vertical_trace = trace_data[:, z_idx]\n        p_picks = adaptive_picker(vertical_trace, dt, phase_type='P')\n\n        # Pick S-wave from horizontal channels\n        try:\n            horizontal_combined = np.sqrt(trace_data[:, e_idx]**2 + trace_data[:, n_idx]**2)\n        except:\n            horizontal_combined = np.abs(trace_data[:, e_idx])\n\n        s_picks = adaptive_picker(horizontal_combined, dt, phase_type='S')\n\n        # If no S-picks found, try vertical channel\n        if len(s_picks) == 0:\n            s_picks = adaptive_picker(vertical_trace, dt, phase_type='S')\n\n        # Limit to reasonable number of picks\n        p_picks = p_picks[:3]  # Max 3 P picks\n        s_picks = s_picks[:3]  # Max 3 S picks\n\n        # Ensure we have at least one pick if possible\n        if len(p_picks) == 0 and len(s_picks) == 0:\n            # Fallback: find any significant energy\n            trace_sq = np.sum(trace_data**2, axis=1)\n            mean_energy = np.mean(trace_sq[:1000])\n            std_energy = np.std(trace_sq[:1000])\n            threshold = mean_energy + 3 * std_energy\n\n            for i in range(1000, len(trace_sq)):\n                if trace_sq[i] > threshold:\n                    if i < 0.4 * len(trace_sq):\n                        p_picks = [i]\n                    else:\n                        s_picks = [i]\n                    break\n\n        # Add picks to results\n        for pick_idx in p_picks:\n            picks.append(('P', pick_idx))\n\n        for pick_idx in s_picks:\n            picks.append(('S', pick_idx))\n\n        return picks\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error processing {file_path}: {e}\")\n        return []\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main function to process all traces.\"\"\"\n    data_dir = '/root/data'\n    results = []\n\n    # Get all npz files\n    npz_files = sorted([f for f in os.listdir(data_dir) if f.endswith('.npz')])\n\n    print(f\"Processing {len(npz_files)} files...\")\n\n    for i, filename in enumerate(npz_files):\n        if (i + 1) % 20 == 0 or i == 0:\n            print(f\"  Processed {i + 1}/{len(npz_files)} files...\")\n\n        file_path = os.path.join(data_dir, filename)\n        picks = process_trace(file_path)\n\n        # Compute squared trace to emphasize arrivals\n    trace_sq = trace ** 2\n\n    # Compute STA and LTA using convolution for speed\n    sta_kernel = np.ones(sta_samples) / sta_samples\n    lta_kernel = np.ones(lta_samples) / lta_samples\n\n    sta = np.convolve(trace_sq, sta_kernel, mode='same')\n    lta = np.convolve(trace_sq, lta_kernel, mode='same')\n\n    # Avoid division by zero\n    lta[lta < 1e-8] = 1e-8\n\n    # Characteristic function\n    char_func = sta / lta\n\n    # Find peaks with minimum distance to avoid multiple picks of same phase\n    min_dist = max(sta_samples, lta_samples) // 4\n\n    # Use different thresholds for P and S waves\n    threshold = threshold_p if sta_samples < 100 else threshold_s\n\n    peaks, _ = signal.find_peaks(char_func, height=threshold, distance=min_dist)\n\n    return peaks\n\ndef adaptive_picker(trace, dt, phase_type='P'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Adaptive picker that adjusts thresholds based on signal characteristics.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Normalize trace\n    trace = trace - np.mean(trace)\n    std_val = np.std(trace)\n    if std_val < 1e-10:\n        return []\n    trace = trace / std_val\n\n    # For P-waves, use vertical channel characteristics\n    if phase_type == 'P':\n        # Shorter windows for P-waves (they have higher frequencies)\n        sta = max(5, int(0.3 / dt))\n        lta = max(50, int(3.0 / dt))\n        threshold = 2.5\n        max_idx = int(0.5 * len(trace))\n    else:  # S-waves\n        # Longer windows for S-waves (lower frequencies)\n        sta = max(10, int(0.8 / dt))\n        lta = max(100, int(8.0 / dt))\n        threshold = 2.0\n        min_idx = int(0.2 * len(trace))\n        max_idx = int(0.9 * len(trace))\n\n    # Get picks using STA/LTA\n    picks = enhanced_sta_lta(trace, sta_samples=sta, lta_samples=lta,\n                            threshold_p=threshold, threshold_s=threshold)\n\n    # Filter picks based on phase type\n    if phase_type == 'P':\n        # P-waves arrive early\n        picks = [p for p in picks if 100 < p < max_idx]\n    else:\n        # S-waves arrive after P-waves\n        picks = [p for p in picks if min_idx < p < max_idx]\n\n    return picks\n\ndef process_trace(file_path):\n    \"\"\"Process a single trace file and return picks.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        data = np.load(file_path)\n        trace_data = data['data']\n        dt = float(data['dt'])\n        channels = str(data['channels']).split(',')\n\n        # Pick P-wave from vertical channel\n        vertical_trace = trace_data[:, z_idx]\n        p_picks = adaptive_picker(vertical_trace, dt, phase_type='P')\n\n        # Pick S-wave from horizontal channels\n        try:\n            horizontal_combined = np.sqrt(trace_data[:, e_idx]**2 + trace_data[:, n_idx]**2)\n        except:\n            horizontal_combined = np.abs(trace_data[:, e_idx])\n\n        s_picks = adaptive_picker(horizontal_combined, dt, phase_type='S')\n\n        # If no S-picks found, try vertical channel\n        if len(s_picks) == 0:\n            s_picks = adaptive_picker(vertical_trace, dt, phase_type='S')\n\n        # Limit to reasonable number of picks\n        p_picks = p_picks[:3]  # Max 3 P picks\n        s_picks = s_picks[:3]  # Max 3 S picks\n\n        # Ensure we have at least one pick if possible\n        if len(p_picks) == 0 and len(s_picks) == 0:\n            # Fallback: find any significant energy\n            trace_sq = np.sum(trace_data**2, axis=1)\n            mean_energy = np.mean(trace_sq[:1000])\n            std_energy = np.std(trace_sq[:1000])\n            threshold = mean_energy + 3 * std_energy\n\n            for i in range(1000, len(trace_sq)):\n                if trace_sq[i] > threshold:\n                    if i < 0.4 * len(trace_sq):\n                        p_picks = [i]\n                    else:\n                        s_picks = [i]\n                    break\n\n        # {"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"05c76df804e8fc15189e48833d1c41f3","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"call_function_y41fp9ln3f6c_1","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/phase_picker_v3.py","content":"import numpy as np\nfrom scipy import signal\nfrom scipy.interpolate import interp1d\nimport os\nimport csv\n\ndef detect_onset_baer(trace, dt, msqrt=10, mavg=5, nthresh=6, pthr=0.3, lthr=0.3):\n    \"\"\"\n    Baer & Kradolfer picker - classic seismological picker.\n    Optimized for P and S wave detection.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Normalize trace\n    trace = trace - np.mean(trace)\n    sigma = np.std(trace)\n    if sigma < 1e-10:\n        return []\n    trace = trace / sigma\n\n    # Compute characteristic function\n    n = len(trace)\n    char_func = np.zeros(n)\n\n    # Use delayed STA/LTA with different windows\n    for i in range(msqrt, n - mavg):\n        # Short window average\n        sta = np.sum(trace[i - msqrt:i + msqrt] ** 2)\n\n        # Long window average\n        lta = np.sum(trace[i - mavg:i - msqrt] ** 2)\n\n        # Avoid division by zero\n        if lta > 0:\n            char_func[i] = sta / (lta ** 0.5)\n\n    # Find peaks in characteristic function\n    peaks, _ = signal.find_peaks(char_func, height=nthresh, distance=int(0.5/dt))\n\n    return peaks\n\ndef polarization_picker(z_trace, e_trace, n_trace, start_idx=0, window=100):\n    \"\"\"\n    Detect S-waves using polarization analysis.\n    S-waves typically show higher horizontal polarization.\n    \"\"\"\n    if len(z_trace) < start_idx + window:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute instantaneous polarization\n    pol_strength = []\n    max_idx = len(z_trace) - window\n\n    for i in range(start_idx, max_idx, 5):  # Skip samples for speed\n        z_win = z_trace[i:i+window]\n        e_win = e_trace[i:i+window]\n        n_win = n_trace[i:i+window]\n\n        # Compute covariance matrix\n        X = np.vstack([z_win, e_win, n_win])\n        cov = np.cov(X)\n\n        # Eigenvalue decomposition\n        eigenvals, eigenvecs = np.linalg.eigh(cov)\n\n        # Sort eigenvalues\n        idx = np.argsort(eigenvals)[::-1]\n        eigenvals = eigenvals[idx]\n\n        # Polarization ratio (how much energy is in horizontal components)\n        if eigenvals[0] > 0:\n            horz_energy = (eigenvals[1] + eigenvals[2]) / eigenvals[0]\n            pol_strength.append((i, horz_energy))\n\n    # Find peaks in polarization strength\n    if len(pol_strength) < 2:\n        return []\n\n    indices = [p[0] for p in pol_strength]\n    values = [p[1] for p in pol_strength]\n\n    # Smooth values\n    values = np.convolve(values, np.ones(5)/5, mode='same')\n\n    # Find peaks\n    peaks, _ = signal.find_peaks(values, height=np.mean(values) + 0.5*np.std(values), distance=20)\n\n    return [indices[p] for p in peaks]\n\ndef energy_ratio_picker(z_trace, e_trace, n_trace, p_pick=None, dt=0.01):\n    \"\"\"\n    Pick S-waves using energy ratio between horizontal and vertical components.\n    S-waves typically have more horizontal energy.\n    \"\"\"\n    n = len(z_trace)\n    if n < 100:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute RMS energy in sliding windows\n    window = int(1.0 / dt)  # 1 second window\n    half_win = window // 2\n\n    z_energy = np.zeros(n)\n    h_energy = np.zeros(n)\n\n    for i in range(half_win, n - half_win):\n        z_win = z_trace[i-half_win:i+half_win]\n        e_win = e_trace[i-half_win:i+half_win]\n        n_win = n_trace[i-half_win:i+half_win]\n\n        z_energy[i] = np.sqrt(np.mean(z_win**2))\n        h_energy[i] = np.sqrt(np.mean(e_win**2 + n_win**2))\n\n    # Filter S-picks to be after P-pick\n        s_picks = [s for s in s_picks if s > first_p + int(0.2/dt)]\n    elif len(s_picks) > 0:\n        # If no P-picks, keep earliest S-picks but require minimum delay from start\n        s_picks = [s for s in s_picks if s > int(5.0/dt)]\n\n    # Limit picks per file\n    p_picks = p_picks[:3]\n    s_picks = s_picks[:3]\n\n    # If we have no P-picks, try more aggressive search\n    if len(p_picks) == 0:\n        cf_z_p_agg = compute_sta_lta(z, dt, sta_samples=max(5, int(0.3/dt)),\n                                     lta_samples=max(50, int(3.0/dt)))\n        p_candidates_agg = find_peaks_cf(cf_z_p_agg, threshold=4.0, min_distance=30)\n        p_picks = [p for p in p_candidates_agg if p < int(0.7 * len(z))][:2]\n\n    # Pick phases\n        p_picks, s_picks = pick_phases(trace, dt)\n\n        # Format results\n        results = []\n        for p in p_picks:\n            results.append(('P', p))\n        for s in s_picks:\n            results.append(('S', s))\n\n        return results\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        return []\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main function.\"\"\"\n    data_dir = '/root/data'\n    results = []\n\n    npz_files = sorted([f for f in os.listdir(data_dir) if f.endswith('.npz')])\n\n    print(f\"Processing {len(npz_files)} files...\")\n\n    for i, filename in enumerate(npz_files):\n        if (i + 1) % 20 == 0 or i == 0:\n            print(f\"  Processed {i + 1}/{len(npz_files)} files...\")\n\n        file_path = os.path.join(data_dir, filename)\n        picks = process_trace(file_path)\n\n        for phase, idx in picks:\n            results.append({\n                'file_name': filename,\n                'phase': phase,\n                'pick_idx': idx\n            })\n\n    # {"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"05c76f279129526c9ed7ba733f164114","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"call_function_8xc4immlmdx9_1","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/phase_picker_aggressive.py","content":"import numpy as np\nfrom scipy import signal\nimport os\nimport csv\n\ndef simple_sta_lta(trace, sta_len, lta_len, threshold=2.0):\n    \"\"\"Simple and fast STA/LTA.\"\"\"\n    trace = trace - np.mean(trace)\n    std = np.std(trace)\n    if std < 1e-12:\n        return np.array([])\n    trace = trace / std\n\n    # Squared trace\n    trace_sq = trace ** 2\n\n    # Compute moving averages\n    sta = np.convolve(trace_sq, np.ones(sta_len)/sta_len, mode='same')\n    lta = np.convolve(trace_sq, np.ones(lta_len)/lta_len, mode='same')\n\n    # Characteristic function\n    cf = sta / (lta + 1e-12)\n\n    # Find peaks\n    min_dist = max(sta_len, lta_len) // 5\n    peaks, _ = signal.find_peaks(cf, height=threshold, distance=min_dist)\n\n    return peaks\n\ndef detect_all_peaks(trace_data, dt):\n    \"\"\"Detect all possible peaks with multiple thresholds.\"\"\"\n    z = trace_data[:, 2]\n    e = trace_data[:, 0]\n    n = trace_data[:, 1]\n    h = np.sqrt(e**2 + n**2)\n\n    all_picks = {'P': [], 'S': []}\n\n    # Try multiple thresholds for vertical channel (P-waves)\n    for threshold in [1.5, 2.0, 2.5, 3.0, 4.0, 5.0]:\n        picks = simple_sta_lta(z, int(0.3/dt), int(3.0/dt), threshold)\n        if len(picks) > 0:\n            all_picks['P'].extend(picks)\n\n    # Try multiple thresholds for horizontal channel (S-waves)\n    for threshold in [1.5, 2.0, 2.5, 3.0, 4.0, 5.0]:\n        picks = simple_sta_lta(h, int(0.5/dt), int(5.0/dt), threshold)\n        if len(picks) > 0:\n            all_picks['S'].extend(picks)\n\n    # Also try vertical for S-waves\n    for threshold in [2.0, 2.5, 3.0, 4.0]:\n        picks = simple_sta_lta(z, int(0.5/dt), int(5.0/dt), threshold)\n        if len(picks) > 0:\n            all_picks['S'].extend(picks)\n\n    return all_picks\n\ndef filter_and_select_picks(picks_dict, trace_length):\n    \"\"\"Filter picks and select the best ones.\"\"\"\n    p_picks = sorted(set(picks_dict['P']))\n    s_picks = sorted(set(picks_dict['S']))\n\n    # Filter P-picks: can be anywhere\n    p_picks = [p for p in p_picks if 50 < p < int(0.8 * trace_length)]\n\n    # Filter S-picks: should be after P-picks if they exist\n    if len(p_picks) > 0:\n        first_p = p_picks[0]\n        s_picks = [s for s in s_picks if s > first_p + int(0.2/dt := 0.01) and s < int(0.9 * trace_length)]\n    else:\n        s_picks = [s for s in s_picks if s > int(2.0/dt := 0.01) and s < int(0.9 * trace_length)]\n\n    # Select top picks\n    # For P: take up to 3 earliest picks\n    p_picks = p_picks[:3]\n\n    # For S: take up to 3 earliest picks\n    s_picks = s_picks[:3]\n\n    # If no S-picks, use a fallback: find strongest signal in horizontal\n    if len(s_picks) == 0 and len(p_picks) > 0:\n        # Look for energy increase after P-pick\n        first_p = p_picks[0]\n        z = np.zeros(trace_length)  # Placeholder\n        # We'll do this in process_trace\n\n    return p_picks, s_picks\n\ndef process_trace(filepath):\n    \"\"\"Process a single trace.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        data = np.load(filepath)\n        trace = data['data']\n        dt = float(data['dt'])\n\n        # Get all picks\n        picks_dict = detect_all_peaks(trace, dt)\n\n        # Filter and select\n        p_picks, s_picks = filter_and_select_picks(picks_dict, len(trace))\n\n        # If we still have no picks, try a fallback\n        if len(p_picks) == 0 and len(s_picks) == 0:\n            # {"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"05c76f7497b04c67606fdf5a25e1d919","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"call_function_nnga6sngccj4_1","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/phase_picker_refined.py","content":"import numpy as np\nfrom scipy import signal\nimport os\nimport csv\n\ndef simple_sta_lta(trace, sta_len, lta_len, threshold=2.0):\n    \"\"\"Simple and fast STA/LTA.\"\"\"\n    trace = trace - np.mean(trace)\n    std = np.std(trace)\n    if std < 1e-12:\n        return np.array([])\n    trace = trace / std\n\n    # Squared trace\n    trace_sq = trace ** 2\n\n    # Compute moving averages\n    sta = np.convolve(trace_sq, np.ones(sta_len)/sta_len, mode='same')\n    lta = np.convolve(trace_sq, np.ones(lta_len)/lta_len, mode='same')\n\n    # Characteristic function\n    cf = sta / (lta + 1e-12)\n\n    # Find peaks\n    min_dist = max(sta_len, lta_len) // 5\n    peaks, _ = signal.find_peaks(cf, height=threshold, distance=min_dist)\n\n    return peaks\n\ndef detect_peaks_multi_threshold(trace, dt, phase_type='P'):\n    \"\"\"Detect peaks using multiple thresholds.\"\"\"\n    all_picks = []\n\n    if phase_type == 'P':\n        # Try multiple STA/LTA configurations for P-waves\n        configs = [\n            (int(0.2/dt), int(2.0/dt), 2.0),\n            (int(0.3/dt), int(3.0/dt), 2.5),\n            (int(0.5/dt), int(5.0/dt), 3.0),\n            (int(0.5/dt), int(5.0/dt), 2.0),\n        ]\n    else:  # S-waves\n        # Try multiple STA/LTA configurations for S-waves\n        configs = [\n            (int(0.5/dt), int(5.0/dt), 2.0),\n            (int(1.0/dt), int(10.0/dt), 2.5),\n            (int(0.5/dt), int(5.0/dt), 3.0),\n        ]\n\n    for sta_len, lta_len, threshold in configs:\n        picks = simple_sta_lta(trace, sta_len, lta_len, threshold)\n        if len(picks) > 0:\n            all_picks.extend(picks)\n\n    return sorted(set(all_picks))\n\ndef process_trace(filepath):\n    \"\"\"Process a single trace.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        data = np.load(filepath)\n        trace = data['data']\n        dt = float(data['dt'])\n\n        z = trace[:, 2]\n        e = trace[:, 0]\n        n = trace[:, 1]\n        h = np.sqrt(e**2 + n**2)\n\n        # Detect P-picks from vertical channel\n        p_candidates = detect_peaks_multi_threshold(z, dt, phase_type='P')\n\n        # Detect S-picks from horizontal channel\n        s_candidates = detect_peaks_multi_threshold(h, dt, phase_type='S')\n\n        # Also try vertical for S-picks (sometimes S-waves are clearer on Z)\n        s_candidates_z = detect_peaks_multi_threshold(z, dt, phase_type='S')\n        s_candidates = sorted(set(list(s_candidates) + list(s_candidates_z)))\n\n        # Filter P-picks: must be at least 5 seconds from start\n        p_picks = [p for p in p_candidates if p >= int(5.0 / dt)]\n\n        # Filter S-picks: must be at least 5 seconds from start\n        s_picks = [s for s in s_candidates if s >= int(5.0 / dt)]\n\n        # Ensure S-picks come after P-picks when both exist\n        if len(p_picks) > 0 and len(s_picks) > 0:\n            first_p = p_picks[0]\n            min_s_idx = first_p + int(0.2 / dt)  # At least 0.2s after P\n            s_picks = [s for s in s_picks if s > min_s_idx]\n        elif len(s_picks) > 0:\n            # If only S-picks, ensure they're not too early\n            s_picks = [s for s in s_picks if s >= int(5.0 / dt)]\n\n        # Remove P and S picks that are too close\n        final_p_picks = []\n        final_s_picks = []\n\n        for p in p_picks:\n            too_close = False\n            for s in s_picks:\n                if abs(p - s) < int(0.5 / dt):  # At least 0.5s apart\n                    too_close = True\n                    break\n            if not too_close:\n                final_p_picks.append(p)\n\n        for s in s_picks:\n            too_close = False\n            for p in final_p_picks:\n                if abs(s - p) < int(0.5 / dt):\n                    too_close = True\n                    break\n            if not too_close:\n                final_s_picks.append(s)\n\n        # Limit to reasonable number of picks\n        p_picks = final_p_picks[:2]  # Max 2 P picks\n        s_picks = final_s_picks[:2]  # Max 2 S picks\n\n        # If we still have no picks, try a fallback approach\n        if len(p_picks) == 0 and len(s_picks) == 0:\n            #
